For DBSCAN with custom metric, we need to use the metric parameter\n        # But sklearn doesn't support custom callables directly in older versions\n        # We'll use the precomputed distance matrix approach\n\n        # Round shape_weight to avoid floating point issues\n    shape_weight_range = np.round(shape_weight_range, 1)\n\n    logger.info(f\"Hyperparameter grid:\")\n    logger.info(f\"  min_samples: {min_samples_range}\")\n    logger.info(f\"  epsilon: {epsilon_range}\")\n    logger.info(f\"  shape_weight: {shape_weight_range}\")\n\n    total_combinations = len(min_samples_range) * len(epsilon_range) * len(shape_weight_range)\n    logger.info(f\"Total combinations to evaluate: {total_combinations}\")\n\n    # Use up to 8 workers\n    logger.info(f\"Using {n_workers} workers\")\n\n    results = []\n    with Pool(n_workers) as pool:\n        for i, result in enumerate(pool.imap_unordered(evaluate_hyperparameters, param_combinations)):\n            results.append(result)\n            if (i + 1) % 10 == 0:\n                logger.info(f\"Completed {i + 1}/{total_combinations} evaluations\")\n\n    # Find Pareto frontier\n    # A point is Pareto-optimal if no other point has both higher F1 and lower delta\n    pareto_points = []\n\n    for idx, row in filtered_df.iterrows():\n        is_dominated = False\n        for idx2, row2 in filtered_df.iterrows():\n            if idx == idx2:\n                continue\n            # Select and order columns\n    pareto_df = pareto_df[['F1', 'delta', 'min_samples', 'epsilon', 'shape_weight']]\n\n    # Write to CSV\n    output_path = '/root/pareto_frontier.csv'\n    pareto_df.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n    logger.info(f\"Pareto frontier saved to {output_path}\")\n\n    # Define hyperparameter grid\n    min_samples_range = list(range(3, 10))  # 3-9\n    epsilon_range = list(range(4, 26, 2))   # 4, 6, 8, ..., 24\n    shape_weight_range = np.arange(0.9, 2.0, 0.1)  # 0.9, 1.0, ..., 1.9\n\n    # Round shape_weight to avoid floating point issues\n    shape_weight_range = np.round(shape_weight_range, 1)\n\n    logger.info(f\"Hyperparameter grid:\")\n    logger.info(f\"  min_samples: {min_samples_range}\")\n    logger.info(f\"  epsilon: {epsilon_range}\")\n    logger.info(f\"  shape_weight: {shape_weight_range}\")\n\n    total_combinations = len(min_samples_range) * len(epsilon_range) * len(shape_weight_range)\n    logger.info(f\"Total combinations to evaluate: {total_combinations}\")\n\n    # Use up to 8 workers\n    logger.info(f\"Using {n_workers} workers\")\n\n    results = []\n    with Pool(n_workers) as pool:\n        for i, result in enumerate(pool.imap_unordered(evaluate_hyperparameters, param_combinations)):\n            results.append(result)\n            if (i + 1) % 10 == 0:\n                logger.info(f\"Completed {i + 1}/{total_combinations} evaluations\")\n\n    # Convert to dataframe\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame(results, columns=['min_samples', 'epsilon', 'shape_weight', 'F1', 'delta'])\n\n    logger.info(f\"Total results: {len(results_df)}\")\n\n    # Filter by F1 > 0.5\n    filtered_df = results_df[results_df['F1'] > 0.5].copy()\n    logger.info(f\"Results with F1 > 0.5: {len(filtered_df)}\")\n\n    # Find Pareto frontier\n    # A point is Pareto-optimal if no other point has both higher F1 and lower delta\n    pareto_points = []\n\n    for idx, row in filtered_df.iterrows():\n        is_dominated = False\n        for idx2, row2 in filtered_df.iterrows():\n            if idx == idx2:\n                continue\n            # row2 dominates row if row2 has higher F1 AND lower delta\n            if row2['F1'] > row['F1'] and row2['delta'] < row['delta']:\n                is_dominated = True\n                break\n\n        if not is_dominated:\n            pareto_points.append(row)\n\n    pareto_df = pd.DataFrame(pareto_points)\n    logger.info(f\"Pareto frontier points: {len(pareto_df)}\")\n\n    # {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_01QrZdiC1BzSRRpN73Uecciu","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01XRcT6X4RPXHudn3BUBehiw","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/dbscan_optimization_v2.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nDBSCAN hyperparameter optimization for Mars cloud clustering (optimized).\nFinds the Pareto frontier of F1 score vs. delta (avg Euclidean distance).\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom sklearn.cluster import DBSCAN\nfrom scipy.spatial.distance import cdist\nimport warnings\nfrom itertools import product\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool, cpu_count\nimport logging\n\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format='%(asctime)s - %(message)s')\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n# Global data to be shared across processes\nGLOBAL_CITSCI_DATA = None\nGLOBAL_EXPERT_DATA = None\n\n\ndef init_worker(citsci_df, expert_df):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker process with data.\"\"\"\n    global GLOBAL_CITSCI_DATA, GLOBAL_EXPERT_DATA\n    GLOBAL_CITSCI_DATA = citsci_df\n    GLOBAL_EXPERT_DATA = expert_df\n\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load citizen science and expert annotations.\"\"\"\n    citsci_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/citsci_train.csv')\n    expert_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/expert_train.csv')\n\n    # Extract relevant columns\n    citsci_df = citsci_df[['file_rad', 'x', 'y']].copy()\n    expert_df = expert_df[['file_rad', 'x', 'y']].copy()\n\n    logger.info(f\"Loaded {len(citsci_df)} citizen science annotations\")\n    logger.info(f\"Loaded {len(expert_df)} expert annotations\")\n\n    return citsci_df, expert_df\n\n\ndef custom_distance_metric(a, b, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute custom weighted Euclidean distance.\n    d(a, b) = sqrt((w * Δx)² + ((2 - w) * Δy)²)\n    \"\"\"\n    dx = a[0] - b[0]\n    dy = a[1] - b[1]\n    return np.sqrt((shape_weight * dx)**2 + ((2 - shape_weight) * dy)**2)\n\n\ndef get_cluster_centroids(points):\n    \"\"\"Compute centroids for each cluster label.\"\"\"\n    if len(points) == 0:\n        return np.array([])\n\n    unique_labels = np.unique(points[:, 2])\n    centroids = []\n\n    for label in unique_labels:\n        if label == -1:  # Skip noise points\n            continue\n        cluster_points = points[points[:, 2] == label, :2]\n        centroid = cluster_points.mean(axis=0)\n        centroids.append(centroid)\n\n    return np.array(centroids) if centroids else np.array([])\n\n\ndef greedy_matching(cluster_centroids, expert_points, max_distance=100):\n    \"\"\"\n    Greedy matching of cluster centroids to expert points.\n    Returns (matches, distances).\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(cluster_centroids) == 0 or len(expert_points) == 0:\n        return [], []\n\n    # Compute distance matrix\n    dist_matrix = cdist(cluster_centroids, expert_points, metric='euclidean')\n\n    matches = []\n    distances = []\n    used_experts = set()\n    used_centroids = set()\n\n    # Find matches by processing smallest distances first\n    flat_indices = np.argsort(dist_matrix.flatten())\n\n    for idx in flat_indices:\n        centroid_idx = idx // len(expert_points)\n        expert_idx = idx % len(expert_points)\n\n        if centroid_idx in used_centroids or expert_idx in used_experts:\n            continue\n        if dist_matrix[centroid_idx, expert_idx] > max_distance:\n            break\n\n        matches.append((centroid_idx, expert_idx))\n        distances.append(dist_matrix[centroid_idx, expert_idx])\n        used_centroids.add(centroid_idx)\n        used_experts.add(expert_idx)\n\n    return matches, distances\n\n\ndef compute_f1_and_delta(cluster_centroids, expert_points):\n    \"\"\"Compute F1 score and average delta.\"\"\"\n    matches, distances = greedy_matching(cluster_centroids, expert_points, max_distance=100)\n\n    if len(matches) == 0:\n        return 0.0, np.nan\n\n    n_clusters = len(cluster_centroids)\n    n_experts = len(expert_points)\n\n    if n_clusters == 0 or n_experts == 0:\n        return 0.0, np.nan\n\n    n_matches = len(matches)\n    precision = n_matches / n_clusters\n    recall = n_matches / n_experts\n\n    if precision + recall == 0:\n        f1 = 0.0\n    else:\n        f1 = 2 * (precision * recall) / (precision + recall)\n\n    delta = np.mean(distances) if distances else np.nan\n\n    return f1, delta\n\n\ndef evaluate_hyperparameters(params):\n    \"\"\"\n    Evaluate a single hyperparameter combination.\n    Uses global data instead of reloading each time.\n    \"\"\"\n    min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight = params\n\n    f1_scores = []\n    deltas = []\n\n    # Get unique images from expert dataset\n    unique_images = GLOBAL_EXPERT_DATA['file_rad'].unique()\n\n    for image_id in unique_images:\n        # Get citizen science points for this image\n        citsci_image = GLOBAL_CITSCI_DATA[GLOBAL_CITSCI_DATA['file_rad'] == image_id][['x', 'y']].values\n\n        # Get expert points for this image\n        expert_image = GLOBAL_EXPERT_DATA[GLOBAL_EXPERT_DATA['file_rad'] == image_id][['x', 'y']].values\n\n        # If no citizen science points, F1 = 0 and delta = NaN\n        if len(citsci_image) == 0:\n            f1_scores.append(0.0)\n            continue\n\n        # Run DBSCAN with custom distance metric\n        def custom_metric(u, v):\n            return custom_distance_metric(u, v, shape_weight)\n\n        try:\n            clustering = DBSCAN(eps=epsilon, min_samples=min_samples, metric=custom_metric, n_jobs=1)\n            labels = clustering.fit_predict(citsci_image)\n\n            # Get cluster centroids\n            if len(np.unique(labels)) > 1 or (len(np.unique(labels)) == 1 and np.unique(labels)[0] != -1):\n                points_with_labels = np.column_stack([citsci_image, labels])\n                cluster_centroids = get_cluster_centroids(points_with_labels)\n            else:\n                cluster_centroids = np.array([])\n\n            # Compute F1 and delta\n            f1, delta = compute_f1_and_delta(cluster_centroids, expert_image)\n            f1_scores.append(f1)\n            if not np.isnan(delta):\n                deltas.append(delta)\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            f1_scores.append(0.0)\n\n    # Compute averages\n    avg_f1 = np.mean(f1_scores) if f1_scores else 0.0\n    avg_delta = np.mean(deltas) if deltas else np.nan\n\n    return min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight, avg_f1, avg_delta\n\n\ndef main():\n    # Load data once\n    citsci_df, expert_df = load_data()\n\n    # Define hyperparameter grid\n    min_samples_range = list(range(3, 10))  # 3-9\n    epsilon_range = list(range(4, 26, 2))   # 4, 6, 8, ..., 24\n    shape_weight_range = np.arange(0.9, 2.0, 0.1)  # 0.9, 1.0, ..., 1.9\n\n    # Round shape_weight to avoid floating point issues\n    shape_weight_range = np.round(shape_weight_range, 1)\n\n    logger.info(f\"Hyperparameter grid:\")\n    logger.info(f\"  min_samples: {min_samples_range}\")\n    logger.info(f\"  epsilon: {epsilon_range}\")\n    logger.info(f\"  shape_weight: {shape_weight_range}\")\n\n    total_combinations = len(min_samples_range) * len(epsilon_range) * len(shape_weight_range)\n    logger.info(f\"Total combinations to evaluate: {total_combinations}\")\n\n    # Extract relevant columns\n    citsci_df = citsci_df[['file_rad', 'x', 'y']].copy()\n    expert_df = expert_df[['file_rad', 'x', 'y']].copy()\n\n    logger.info(f\"Loaded {len(citsci_df)} citizen science annotations\")\n    logger.info(f\"Loaded {len(expert_df)} expert annotations\")\n\n    return citsci_df, expert_df\n\n\ndef custom_distance_metric(a, b, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute custom weighted Euclidean distance.\n    d(a, b) = sqrt((w * Δx)² + ((2 - w) * Δy)²)\n    \"\"\"\n    dx = a[0] - b[0]\n    dy = a[1] - b[1]\n    return np.sqrt((shape_weight * dx)**2 + ((2 - shape_weight) * dy)**2)\n\n\ndef get_cluster_centroids(points):\n    \"\"\"Compute centroids for each cluster label.\"\"\"\n    if len(points) == 0:\n        return np.array([])\n\n    unique_labels = np.unique(points[:, 2])\n    centroids = []\n\n    for label in unique_labels:\n        if label == -1:  # Skip noise points\n            continue\n        cluster_points = points[points[:, 2] == label, :2]\n        centroid = cluster_points.mean(axis=0)\n        centroids.append(centroid)\n\n    return np.array(centroids) if centroids else np.array([])\n\n\ndef greedy_matching(cluster_centroids, expert_points, max_distance=100):\n    \"\"\"\n    Greedy matching of cluster centroids to expert points.\n    Returns (matches, distances).\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(cluster_centroids) == 0 or len(expert_points) == 0:\n        return [], []\n\n    # Compute distance matrix\n    dist_matrix = cdist(cluster_centroids, expert_points, metric='euclidean')\n\n    matches = []\n    distances = []\n    used_experts = set()\n    used_centroids = set()\n\n    # Find matches by processing smallest distances first\n    flat_indices = np.argsort(dist_matrix.flatten())\n\n    for idx in flat_indices:\n        centroid_idx = idx // len(expert_points)\n        expert_idx = idx % len(expert_points)\n\n        if centroid_idx in used_centroids or expert_idx in used_experts:\n            continue\n        if dist_matrix[centroid_idx, expert_idx] > max_distance:\n            break\n\n        matches.append((centroid_idx, expert_idx))\n        distances.append(dist_matrix[centroid_idx, expert_idx])\n        used_centroids.add(centroid_idx)\n        used_experts.add(expert_idx)\n\n    return matches, distances\n\n\ndef compute_f1_and_delta(cluster_centroids, expert_points):\n    \"\"\"Compute F1 score and average delta.\"\"\"\n    matches, distances = greedy_matching(cluster_centroids, expert_points, max_distance=100)\n\n    if len(matches) == 0:\n        return 0.0, np.nan\n\n    n_clusters = len(cluster_centroids)\n    n_experts = len(expert_points)\n\n    if n_clusters == 0 or n_experts == 0:\n        return 0.0, np.nan\n\n    n_matches = len(matches)\n    precision = n_matches / n_clusters\n    recall = n_matches / n_experts\n\n    if precision + recall == 0:\n        f1 = 0.0\n    else:\n        f1 = 2 * (precision * recall) / (precision + recall)\n\n    delta = np.mean(distances) if distances else np.nan\n\n    return f1, delta\n\n\ndef evaluate_hyperparameters(params):\n    \"\"\"\n    Evaluate a single hyperparameter combination.\n    Uses global data instead of reloading each time.\n    \"\"\"\n    min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight = params\n\n    f1_scores = []\n    deltas = []\n\n    # Get unique images from expert dataset\n    unique_images = GLOBAL_EXPERT_DATA['file_rad'].unique()\n\n    for image_id in unique_images:\n        # Get citizen science points for this image\n        citsci_image = GLOBAL_CITSCI_DATA[GLOBAL_CITSCI_DATA['file_rad'] == image_id][['x', 'y']].values\n\n        # Get expert points for this image\n        expert_image = GLOBAL_EXPERT_DATA[GLOBAL_EXPERT_DATA['file_rad'] == image_id][['x', 'y']].values\n\n        # If no citizen science points, F1 = 0 and delta = NaN\n        if len(citsci_image) == 0:\n            f1_scores.append(0.0)\n            continue\n\n        # Run DBSCAN with custom distance metric\n        def custom_metric(u, v):\n            return custom_distance_metric(u, v, shape_weight)\n\n        try:\n            clustering = DBSCAN(eps=epsilon, min_samples=min_samples, metric=custom_metric, n_jobs=1)\n            labels = clustering.fit_predict(citsci_image)\n\n            # Get cluster centroids\n            if len(np.unique(labels)) > 1 or (len(np.unique(labels)) == 1 and np.unique(labels)[0] != -1):\n                points_with_labels = np.column_stack([citsci_image, labels])\n                cluster_centroids = get_cluster_centroids(points_with_labels)\n            else:\n                cluster_centroids = np.array([])\n\n            # Compute F1 and delta\n            f1, delta = compute_f1_and_delta(cluster_centroids, expert_image)\n            f1_scores.append(f1)\n            if not np.isnan(delta):\n                deltas.append(delta)\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            f1_scores.append(0.0)\n\n    # Compute averages\n    avg_f1 = np.mean(f1_scores) if f1_scores else 0.0\n    avg_delta = np.mean(deltas) if deltas else np.nan\n\n    return min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight, avg_f1, avg_delta\n\n\ndef main():\n    # Load data once\n    citsci_df, expert_df = load_data()\n\n    # Define hyperparameter grid\n    min_samples_range = list(range(3, 10))  # 3-9\n    epsilon_range = list(range(4, 26, 2))   # 4, 6, 8, ..., 24\n    shape_weight_range = np.arange(0.9, 2.0, 0.1)  # 0.9, 1.0, ..., 1.9\n\n    # Round shape_weight to avoid floating point issues\n    shape_weight_range = np.round(shape_weight_range, 1)\n\n    logger.info(f\"Hyperparameter grid:\")\n    logger.info(f\"  min_samples: {min_samples_range}\")\n    logger.info(f\"  epsilon: {epsilon_range}\")\n    logger.info(f\"  shape_weight: {shape_weight_range}\")\n\n    total_combinations = len(min_samples_range) * len(epsilon_range) * len(shape_weight_range)\n    logger.info(f\"Total combinations to evaluate: {total_combinations}\")\n\n    # Generate all parameter combinations\n    param_combinations = list(product(min_samples_range, epsilon_range, shape_weight_range))\n\n    # Parallel evaluation\n    logger.info(\"Starting hyperparameter search...\")\n    n_workers = min(cpu_count(), 8)\n    logger.info(f\"Using {n_workers} workers\")\n\n    results = []\n    with Pool(n_workers, initializer=init_worker, initargs=(citsci_df, expert_df)) as pool:\n        for i, result in enumerate(pool.imap_unordered(evaluate_hyperparameters, param_combinations, chunksize=5)):\n            results.append(result)\n            if (i + 1) % 50 == 0:\n                logger.info(f\"Completed {i + 1}/{total_combinations} evaluations\")\n\n    logger.info(f\"Completed all {total_combinations} evaluations\")\n\n    # Convert to dataframe\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame(results, columns=['min_samples', 'epsilon', 'shape_weight', 'F1', 'delta'])\n\n    logger.info(f\"Total results: {len(results_df)}\")\n\n    # Filter by F1 > 0.5\n    filtered_df = results_df[results_df['F1'] > 0.5].copy()\n    logger.info(f\"Results with F1 > 0.5: {len(filtered_df)}\")\n\n    # Sort by F1 (descending)\n    pareto_df = pareto_df.sort_values('F1', ascending=False).reset_index(drop=True)\n\n    # Round values as specified\n    pareto_df['F1'] = pareto_df['F1'].round(5)\n    pareto_df['delta'] = pareto_df['delta'].round(5)\n    pareto_df['shape_weight'] = pareto_df['shape_weight'].round(1)\n    pareto_df['min_samples'] = pareto_df['min_samples'].astype(int)\n    pareto_df['epsilon'] = pareto_df['epsilon'].astype(int)\n\n    # Select and order columns\n    pareto_df = pareto_df[['F1', 'delta', 'min_samples', 'epsilon', 'shape_weight']]\n\n    # Write to CSV\n    output_path = '/root/pareto_frontier.csv'\n    pareto_df.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n    logger.info(f\"Pareto frontier saved to {output_path}\")\n\n    #
